The backward half-window contrasts measured scarcity with honor-claims, withheld care, consolidated inheritance, and abundant wealth, while 89:22 places lordly arrival and ordered rows above the leveled field. **Mechanism:** crushing removes the vertical differences on which claims to elevated worth and possession rested. The packet supplies social asymmetry, leveling, and a new order; I infer the corrective relation. **Reading change:** the repeated *dakkan* is not generic destruction but a physical abolition of manufactured hierarchy.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000482/B001","root_000482/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9787c89bd831a1fbfe44","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"89:21:3\",\"root_000482\",[\"B001\",\"B008\"]],[\"89:21:5\",\"root_000482\",[\"B001\",\"B008\"]],[\"89:21:6\",\"root_000482\",[\"B001\",\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Özel elde biriktirme, herkesin önce sıkıştırılıp sonra tek tek karşılaşacağı yeni bir düzene yerleştirilmesiyle tersine çevrilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B001","root_000482/B001","root_000482/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a8137a5719620c78794e","text":"1. **The ground that held power is leveled.** Lexical evidence: `د ك ك B001` crushing/demolishing until leveled; `د ك ك B002` flat low spread-outness; `ء ر ض B001` lower ground opposite sky. Structural cue: repeated `دكًّا دكًّا` echoes the earlier repetitions of claims and intensifiers while reversing pillars, rock, and pegs. Assigned role: the earth becomes the surface on which every vertical or fixed power is flattened. Abductive move: I infer that earlier built images are the implied targets of leveling; the packet supplies pillars, rocks, pegs, and earth-crushing. Reading change: the ayah reads as material deconstruction of the previous civilizational grammar.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000482/B008","root_000871/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ae4d0382bf5dc11482da","text":"2. **Private aggregation turned into universal compression.** **Evidence/cue:** `د ك ك B008` is crowding and pressing together, recalling total gathering through `ل م م B001` in 89:19; 89:22 then arranges rows through `ص ف ف B001`, and 89:25-26 twice isolates “anyone” through `ء ح د B002`. **Mechanism:** what people privately gathered is answered by the gathering of people themselves, first compressed and then ordered for individual encounter. The packet supplies all three spatial operations; their sequence is inferred as reversal. **Reading change:** the reduplication makes leveling a social re-sorting process as well as a geological event.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B001","root_000482/B001","root_000482/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_bdacea461b4eaad94dd7","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"89:21:3\",\"root_000482\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]],[\"89:21:4\",\"root_000025\",[\"B001\"]],[\"89:21:5\",\"root_000482\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]],[\"89:21:6\",\"root_000482\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yerin dümdüz edilmesi, sütunlar, kazıklar ve servet üzerinde kurulmuş toplumsal yükselti ve üstünlük iddialarını maddi olarak ortadan kaldırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B006","root_000482/B003","root_000482/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_cdd3655f3d2408b104fa","text":"2. **Burial-covering and crowd-pressure.** Lexical evidence: `د ك ك B003` covering/filling with soil over a dead person; `د ك ك B008` crowding and pressing together; `ء ر ض B006` sticking to ground and lingering. Structural cue: after accumulated eating/loving, the whole earth is pressed repeatedly. Assigned role: the repeated crushing compresses social excess into a final ground-level condition. Abductive move: I infer that the earth-event is both demolition and burial-pressure, because later 89:22-23 bring ordered rows and remembrance after compression. Reading change: the ayah becomes a transition from inflated appetite to pressed exposure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B001","root_000482/B003","root_000482/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d093e8cefb9ef2fc7a94","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"89:21:3\",\"root_000482\",[\"B003\",\"B008\"]],[\"89:21:4\",\"root_000025\",[\"B001\"]],[\"89:21:5\",\"root_000482\",[\"B003\",\"B008\"]],[\"89:21:6\",\"root_000482\",[\"B003\",\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Tekrarlanan ezilme, kabarmış toplumsal fazlalığı toprağa bastıran bir gömme ve örtme basıncı da taşır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
